Plains Cannabis & Hemp Business Directory — Audit and Corrected Report

Region: Iowa, Missouri, Kansas, Nebraska, North Dakota, South Dakota, Oklahoma  |  Last audited: 31 October 2026 (source file dated 30 October 2026)  |  Auditor: separate fact-checking agent; this agent did not write the original report.

Count reconciliation — read first. The source file claims 211 unique main entries. Its tables actually contain 215 rows, of which 2 are Rochester Armored Car cross-references and 1 is a second GrowGeneration row, leaving 212 unique main entries audited. The +1 difference is Category 3: the file claims 9 insurance entries but its table contains 10 (Heffernan, POWERS, Amwins, Scott, MariProtect, Ludovissy, ProAg, RiverStone, HUB, Meyer). Verdicts therefore sum to 212, not 211: Confirmed 52 + Corrected 99 + Unverified 51 + Removed 6 + Already listed 4 = 212. Kept in the rewrite + Already listed + Removed + Hemp licence only / research-stage = 192 + 4 + 6 + 10 = 212. The 10 hemp/research moves are: Midwest Natural Fiber, Hemp Solutions, Saluna, South Bend Industrial Hemp, BastLab, Complete Hemp Processing, Horizon Specialty Seeds, NuDose (probable), Global Fiber Processing (research-stage) and Renewabuild Great Plains (planned). Dakota Cannabis Consulting is counted in Removed and also named in the licensed-operator list because its removal reason is a principal-level licence (see below) — it is not double-counted.

Method and limits — stated plainly

Every entry's own official website was attempted first. Where a text fetch hit a 429 rate limit or a bot-check, that is stated in the audit notes and the entry was verified from search-indexed copies of the own site, directories or press — a 429 is a tool limit, not evidence a site is down, and those entries should get one fresh direct-open pass before republication. State Secretary of State registries (Missouri, Oklahoma, Iowa, Kansas BESS, Nebraska, North Dakota, South Dakota) are interactive/CAPTCHA-gated and returned no direct name-searchable record in this pass for most firms; no SOS status is claimed as confirmed in this audit unless a checker cites a specific record. Bar, CPA, insurance-producer, contractor and security licence numbers are likewise Unverified for almost every entry — no number has been invented, and the few numbers shown as published (Americare OK Mechanical #139597, U.S. Alert Oklahoma State license 774, Cam-Dex MO #954667 / OK #AC1902 / St. Louis County C-0451) still need regulator cross-check for currency and issuer. Missouri DCR and Oklahoma OMMA checks were web-search checks, not direct portal/list downloads (the DCR list is an interactive dashboard; OMMA's database is portal-based), so 'no licence found' is a search-level negative, not a certified clearance — a definitive name-by-name portal check by business and owner name is still required before republication, especially for Oklahoma entries and for the principal-level flags below. Only publicly published business contacts are shown; personal phones/emails are not reproduced. Social links are shown only where a site footer actually exposed them.

Part 1 — Audit table

One row per unique entry, in source order. 'What was checked' gives the business's own site / evidence link from the source file, as opened or attempted in this audit; where the checker states a fetch failed (429, bot-check, timeout, DNS), that is the check result for that entry. Credentials are Confirmed only where the checker cites a published number; otherwise Unverified — see Method above.

Scope, exclusions and same-name traps (as filed, audit-checked)

Included: businesses based in, or with a verified office in, a Plains state, or (where flagged as serving) actively serving cannabis/hemp clients in that state, that are built around cannabis/hemp or show evidence of serving cannabis/hemp clients. Excluded from the main tables: Missouri Division of Cannabis Regulation licensees; Oklahoma Medical Marijuana Authority commercial licensees; Iowa medical cannabidiol manufacturers and dispensaries; North Dakota medical marijuana manufacturing facilities and dispensaries; South Dakota medical cannabis establishments; Nebraska Medical Cannabis Commission licensees; and tribal cannabis enterprises in any of these states. Hemp growers/processors licensed under USDA or a state hemp plan are entries in Category 12 only if they make/brand finished products (see Hemp licence only list). National companies are in the main tables only where a verified Plains office was found, marked National; nationals serving the Plains without a verified Plains office are held in the source Appendix, not counted. Same-name and cross-border traps checked: Kansas City MO vs Kansas City KS kept apart; Springfield MO vs IL/OH; Columbia MO vs SC; Independence MO vs KS; Lawrence KS vs MA; Manhattan KS vs NY; Lincoln NE vs IL; Kearney NE vs MO; Davenport IA vs FL; Aberdeen SD vs WA; Jamestown ND vs NY; Norman vs Moore OK. Cross-border metros: Kansas City MO/KS, St. Louis MO / Metro East IL, Omaha–Council Bluffs NE/IA, Sioux City IA/NE/SD, Fargo–Moorhead ND/MN, Grand Forks ND/MN, Quad Cities IA/IL, Joplin MO/KS/OK, Fort Smith AR/OK. A business is listed under a state only if it has an office there; other states served are noted. Firms that may already appear in the Great Lakes directory (St. Louis Metro East, Quad Cities, Fargo–Moorhead) or the South Central directory (Fort Smith / Northwest Arkansas) are flagged in the audit table as Already listed where the same firm was found.

Source file: Plains_directory_complete.html (30 October 2026), audited 31 October 2026. This file is self-contained; external links are source links, not embedded content. Do not present Unverified entries, SOS status, or licence numbers as confirmed until the follow-up routes in the still-unverified list have been run.
